# Value

Value is the engine's single dynamic value model: one tagged struct covering null, bool, 256-bit int, string, bytes, nanoton Amount, Account, Asset, Cell, Block reference, List, Dict, and Obj, shared by expression evaluation, matcher where predicates, build programs, host parsers, and action serialization. Lists and dicts hold their payload behind shared pointers, so copying a Value is cheap and copies alias the same items and fields; the Block pointer is non-owning and valid only while the owning BlockArena outlives the value. Accounts and jetton masters go through the shared canonicalize-or-passthrough policy: a well-formed raw address becomes uppercase canonical form, anything else is stored verbatim rather than rejected. Two free helpers serialize cells to BOC in the two container modes the pipeline needs (bare for rendering, CRC32C for bytes stored into parsed actions), and an absent Amount is encoded as an Amount whose number is null. All operations here are non-throwing, single-threaded value plumbing with no locking.

## class mch::Value

- decl: `ton-index-worker/ton-mch-engine/src/Value.h:36`

Tagged dynamic value shared by expressions, builds, host parsers, and serialization.

A VType tag selects which members carry the payload and unrelated members stay default. List, Dict, and Obj share one fields or items buffer across copies; Account and Asset keep the canonical address in str with addr_none, is_ton, and has_jetton as discriminators; Amount uses a null num to mean absent. Field lookup is a first-match linear scan, and the Block reference is borrowed from the arena.

Wraps a field list as an Obj value for attribute-style access.

Obj shares the fields payload shape with Dict and only the tag differs; field lookup works identically on both.

Finds a named field in a Dict or Obj; returns nullptr when absent.

Linear first-match scan over the shared fields vector. A value with no fields payload at all, meaning any other type, also returns nullptr rather than erroring.

## boc_with_mode

`td::Result<std::string> boc_with_mode(const td::Ref<vm::Cell> & root, int mode)`

- decl: `ton-index-worker/ton-mch-engine/src/Value.cpp:144`
- callers: mch::td_boc_serialize (ton-index-worker/ton-mch-engine/src/Value.cpp:157); mch::td_boc_serialize_crc (ton-index-worker/ton-mch-engine/src/Value.cpp:161)

Serializes a cell tree to BOC bytes under an explicit container mode.

A null root cell is an error, the underlying std_boc_serialize error propagates unchanged, and success returns the serialized bytes as a string.

Uses mode 0 with no CRC appended — the variant used wherever output is displayed rather than stored.

Serializes a cell to a CRC32C-container BOC for bytes stored into parsed actions.

The CRC mode keeps stored parser fields byte-identical with the older pipeline; cell content is identical to the bare mode, only the container differs.

- decl: `ton-index-worker/ton-mch-engine/src/Value.cpp:164`
- callers: [mch::rt_builtin_account (ton-index-worker/ton-mch-engine/src/ExprRuntime.cpp:433)](ExprRuntime.md#rt_builtin_account); [mch::rt_builtin_asset (ton-index-worker/ton-mch-engine/src/ExprRuntime.cpp:449)](ExprRuntime.md#rt_builtin_asset); [mch::rt_builtin_asset_of (ton-index-worker/ton-mch-engine/src/ExprRuntime.cpp:506)](ExprRuntime.md#rt_builtin_asset_of); [mch::account_eq_str (ton-index-worker/ton-mch-engine/src/ExprRuntime.cpp:60)](ExprRuntime.md#account_eq_str); mch::canonicalize_or_passthrough (ton-index-worker/ton-mch-engine/src/Value.cpp:201); [mch::account_from_opt (ton-index-worker/ton-mch-engine/src/host/HostCommon.cpp:46)](HostCommon.md#account_from_opt)

Normalizes a raw workchain:hex64 address to canonical uppercase form, or nullopt when malformed.

The workchain part must be an optionally negative run of digits and the account id exactly 64 hex digits; anything else, including user-friendly base64 forms, returns nullopt. addr_none is not handled here.

Renders a short diagnostic string used in vector FAIL lines.

Every type gets a compact tag: null, true or false, int and amount with nan for null numbers, the str text, a bytes length, account and asset forms, cell, Block, and recursing renders for lists, dicts, and objects. Diagnostics only — not a serialization format.

- calls: `mch::Value::Value`, `mch::canonicalize_or_passthrough`

Makes a jetton Asset from a raw master address.

The master address runs through canonicalize_or_passthrough, so a well-formed raw address is uppercased and a malformed string is stored verbatim.

- calls: `mch::Value::Value`, `mch::canonicalize_or_passthrough`

Makes an Account from a raw address string.

canonicalize_or_passthrough stores the uppercase wc:HEX64 form for valid input and keeps a malformed string as given rather than rejecting, so callers must not assume canonical output.
